# Complete 144-point wholesale price benchmark

Prices and errors are AUD/kWh. Each forecast covers five-minute **interval starts** at decision time plus 0, 5, ..., 715 minutes. The +0 settlement is a label only. Results use 43,909 identical July–November 2025 common origins for every horizon. This is reused development evaluation, not an independent test. The held-out summer period was not read.

The original 41 decision-time-safe columns, including +60-aligned historical seasonal inputs, remain frozen. Direct models can train on horizon-specific eligible rows; common-origin tuning and all reported evaluation scores use the same full-trajectory intersection. The 72-row TCN excludes 71 additional early training origins. Ten frozen direct XGBoost models were reused after exact feature, parameter and label matching; 134 were fitted with the same V2-A settings.

## Equal-horizon mean MAE

| Model | January–June tuning | Reused July–November evaluation |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| persistence | 0.12190 | 0.07777 |
| daily_seasonal | 0.09799 | 0.05290 |
| train_median | 0.08310 | 0.06061 |
| hour_of_week_median | 0.07345 | 0.04871 |
| direct | 0.06277 | 0.03804 |
| hybrid_tuned | 0.06267 | 0.03809 |
| hybrid_fixed | 0.06268 | 0.03806 |
| tcn | 0.06579 | 0.04080 |

The lowest tuning mean is **hybrid_tuned**. The tuned hybrid selects persistence at 3 offsets and direct XGBoost at 141; choices were frozen before reused evaluation. The fixed alternative uses persistence at +0/+5 and direct at the other 142 offsets.

## Computation

Direct: 10 reused + 134 new models; new-fit time **4139.9 s**, summed time across all 144 historical fits **4360.8 s**, combined saved size **412.1 MiB**. **10** direct models selected the final permitted boosting round, so their best attainable tuning score under a larger budget is unknown. TCN: **13,656 parameters**, selected epoch **5**, fit **309.3 s**, checkpoint **60,541 bytes**, training process peak **2115.2 MiB**. Evaluation process peak **2721.0 MiB**.

| Model | Prepared 144-point median ms | P95 ms |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| direct | 151.283 | 162.515 |
| hybrid_fixed | 147.486 | 170.353 |
| hybrid_tuned | 151.555 | 169.121 |
| tcn | 1.918 | 2.231 |

Latency excludes model loading and feature construction. These are local CPU measurements, not a live-service guarantee. Learning curves, timestamps, fingerprints, all monthly and conditional values, and compressed matched prediction matrices are saved under `models/price_full_horizon_v1/`.

The completed trajectory supplies a shape-compatible offline price forecast for eventual 12-hour MPC research, but it has not been fed into MPC. Historical AEMO revisions and the optimistic completed-price publication assumption still limit live validity. No predispatch source, held-out summer data, RL or hardware was accessed.
